",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"artifact_changed"}]," SSE event。这样即使一次 POST 触发 upsert 和 eviction，client 也能收到 created/updated 以及 removed 的完整增量。同一次 mutation
